# AI prompt log, 2026-11

Format and rules: see `.claude/CLAUDE.md`. From 2026-11-01, code is worked, reviewed and deployed on a local
`dev` branch, and GitHub main gets at most two squashed commits a day. Each entry cites the dev commits and the
squash commit that carries them. The full dev-to-squash map is `research-log/dev-squash-map.txt`.

> worker killed during the wait leaves a record that reconcile can book; (h) pass the caller's price into the stop
> place/restore calls so tick rounding can keep a SELL stop below the market.

> replay_spill.

  double booking after one failure); these were fixed. Re-reviews of each follow-up came back SAFE: the
  quantity-aware repair, fill-wait SELLs counted as pending, the guarded late mark, and the one-day bound.
- Five reviews in all. On dev, not deployed yet; the deploy is planned for the 11-03 evening stop.

## 2026-11-03 10:39 EST (first commit): Stop restores and coverage fixes over a slow or refused cancel (M230 and follow-ups)

**Luke (original, verbatim):**
> if you finish all actionable items start to think and research about how we could make this bot better, and then act on it without me involved.

(Professor pre-deploy review item M230, plus follow-ups from fresh agent reviews and the professor's add-on review; written by trading-bot-ee, Claude.)

**Request drafted by:** AI, under the standing instruction quoted above (the item named in the refined block). Luke's words are the instruction, not this specific request. Relabelled 2026-11-06 (professor M317).

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: a restore over a stop whose cancel is slow, or that reverts, must not leave a double stop or a bare position. _fix_overstop must never count a cancelling stop as cover. A cross-client stop that refuses a cancel must not cause a cancel/replace loop. Alerts must not hide a safety 🚨. A cancel must never hit another ticker's order with the same orderId.
> Scope / files: execution.py, tasks.py, risk_manager.py, ibkr.py, main.py, tests.
> Constraints: under-cover is worse than over-cover; no threshold or risk-limit change; old Celery messages stay compatible.
> Out of scope (do not touch): the policy for a refused stop larger than the position (packet H22, Luke).
> Acceptance check: suite passes; each commit reviewed SAFE by a fresh agent; professor pre-deploy review SAFE.
> Open questions for Luke: H22 (options only).

**Done:**
- The restore re-reads after 5 s when a stop is cancelling, and sizes to min(cache, fresh).
- A new tasks.recheck_stop_coverage runs under the ticker lock: up to 6 tries, with a fresh-vs-cache position cross-check.
- _fix_overstop waits for both cancelling statuses and sizes from working stops only.
- Stops that refuse a cancel are remembered by permId (stop_cancel_refused_<T>), so they aren't cancelled again.
- Refused stops larger than the position get an unthrottled 🚨 and one cancel retry.
- The alert throttles are split.
- cancel_order checks symbol and permId.
- The PT-hold wording is fixed.
- An order-dependent test is fixed.
- One version (8966f40) was blocked in review for a cancel/replace loop and fixed before merge.

- execution.py (AI-edited: `_restore_cover`, `_restore_sized_stop`, `_restore_stop_after_failed_sell`; `_queue_coverage_recheck` AI-generated).

## 2026-11-03 17:29 EST (commit): Stop coverage re-check, flat and sign-flip reads

**Request (the 30-minute improve-loop cron prompt that Luke set up, run by Claude (trading-bot-ee); not a new prompt from Luke; findings from the professor's 11:40 add-on review (m327, m329-m331), the 12:12 review (m339) and run 42 at 12:47 (m345, m354)):**
> [Helios improve loop, every 30 min] You are trading-bot-ee, lead of the Helios box ... Work the top open item in roadmap.md ... (excerpt)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: no SELL stop is ever placed on a short. A fresh read that leaves out a ticker or disagrees on sign must not size a stop from the cache, and the alert must say what happened.
> Scope / files: execution.py, tasks.py, risk_manager.py, tests.
> Constraints: under-cover is worse than over-cover; no stop-level or risk-limit change.
> Out of scope (do not touch): M237, the policy for a refused stop larger than the position (Luke, packet H22).
> Acceptance check: tests for flat, missing-ticker and sign-flip reads; professor SAFE.

**Done:**
- The coverage re-check does nothing on a fresh read that leaves out the ticker and queues another look within the cap. Reads that disagree on sign size to 0.
- _restore_sized_stop places nothing and returns "sign_flip" when a fresh read shows a short. That sends the throttled sign-flip alert and queues the restore. The queued retry and reconcile's auto-stop check the sign before any SELL stop. The give-up alert says to check long or short in TWS.
- Reviewed SAFE (12:12 and run 42).

## 2026-11-03 17:29 EST (commit): Vector store resume, dead-letter and canary (continues the 11-03 09:47 01n entry)

**Request (the 30-minute improve-loop cron prompt that Luke set up, run by Claude (trading-bot-ee); not a new prompt from Luke; findings from the professor's 11:40 review (M238, M239), the 12:12 review (M240, m335-m338, m340, m341) and run 42 (m344, m347)):**
> [Helios improve loop, every 30 min] You are trading-bot-ee, lead of the Helios box ... Work the top open item in roadmap.md ... (excerpt)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: the 3-failure bound on rebuild resumes holds even when a process is killed mid-upsert. One bad record can no longer pause prune forever, and a store that takes no writes never sets records aside.
> Scope / files: vector_store.py, tasks.py, tests.
> Constraints: no document is dropped silently (set-asides go to a dead-letter file and alert); retrieval for the analyst unchanged.
> Out of scope (do not touch): the resume age filter. It was built, then removed, because it changes analyst input (Luke's).
> Acceptance check: tests for kills, progress, isolated failures, canary, cap; professor SAFE.
> Open questions for Luke: none.

**Done:**
- Every resume is counted before it starts and reset on progress (records landed).
- A record failing three resumes in a row is dead-lettered, but only when the store is shown to take writes: another upsert landed, or a scratch canary record (written, read back, deleted) passes. At most min(10, max(1, n//100)) are set aside per run. A final set-aside check runs once when resumes stop.
- The canary is never searched, rebuilt or kept. Set-asides alert once a day per collection, and the held counts are flushed.

**Files changed:** `vector_store.py` (AI-edited: `_resume_attempt`, `rebuild_collection`, `prune_old_documents`, `pending_rebuilds`, `search`, `_read_collection_docs`, `_count_collection_rows`; AI-generated: `_past_max_age`, `_age_cutoff`, `_deadletter_path`, `_dead_letter`, `_canary_upsert`, `_isolated_cap`, `_fsync_dir`, `_set_aside_after_giving_up`, `PRUNE_MAX_AGE_DAYS`), `tasks.py` (AI-edited: `_log_vector_store_rebuild`, `_rebuild_and_log`, `prune_vector_store`, `_alert_unfinished_rebuilds`; AI-generated: `_alert_set_aside`), tests (AI-edited): `tests/test_vector_store_rebuild_recovery.py`, `tests/test_vector_store_single_view.py`

## 2026-11-03 17:29 EST (commit): Buy rules, a 30% micro-tier per-name cap and a screener new_fact buy gate

**Request (drafted by Jarvis, claude-jarvis@pc, bus #186 and #189, relaying Luke's decisions; not Luke's own words. Summary, not the bus text):**
> #186: on the micro tier, cap any one name at 30% of equity for new buys (never sell to get under it). Add a new_fact field to the 7B screener output (new factual information vs opinion or recap), and let an OPEN/ADD route only when a screener new fact is behind it.
> #189: gate scope news_only; window 24h.

(This follows audit 2026-11-03 15:23 findings 2 and 3: the 11-03 buy came from an opinion piece, and the single-name cap was unenforced.)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: build both buy rules as Luke set them, applied wherever a buy is sized or re-sized, with a route row for every block.
> Scope / files: risk_manager.py, agents/crew.py, agents/screener.py (prompt and parser), agents/c6_shadow.py, config.py, db.py, tasks.py, helios3d/replay/harness.py, tests.

> Out of scope (do not touch): other tiers; trims of existing holdings.

**Done:**
- micro_name_cap cuts or blocks a micro-tier OPEN/ADD (ROUTE_MICRO_NAME_CAP). The screener records new_fact. new_fact_gate blocks a buy without one (ROUTE_NO_NEW_FACT), with defaults news_only and 24h per #189.
- Review fixes: M256 (the held read fails closed, including a stop record with no broker position, which alerts once a day), M257 (SWAP INCOMPLETE alert after a post-sale block, 0.5 pp swap slack), M259 (a typo keeps the gate on and alerts once), plus m421, m422 and m425.

## 2026-11-03 21:53 EST (commit): Outcome close row locking; orphan sweep skips in-flight sells

**Request (the 30-minute improve-loop cron prompt that Luke set up, run by Claude (trading-bot-ee); not a new prompt from Luke; follow-up (b) in roadmap 01zg (the close_trade_outcome open-rows SELECT has no FOR UPDATE), then m462 and m463 from the professor's reviews of next-2026-11-07):**
> [Helios improve loop, every 30 min] You are trading-bot-ee, lead of the Helios box ... Work the top open item in roadmap.md ... (excerpt)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: two concurrent closes of the same ticker can't overwrite each other. A close that loses a lock race is retried. The orphan sweep doesn't flag a row whose real close is in flight.
> Scope / files: db.py `close_trade_outcome`, `flag_orphan_outcomes`, tests.
> Constraints: no row lock or pooled connection held during the SPY price fetch; failures return False, never raise.
> Out of scope (do not touch): m465 (a queued flat exit waiting over 30 minutes; an orders-row write failure), left open.
> Acceptance check: tests; professor SAFE (ledger 44e-44h).
> Open questions for Luke: none.

**Done:**
- close_trade_outcome looks at the open rows unlocked, fetches the SPY exit price, then locks them (FOR UPDATE) and updates only rows still open. It retries once after a deadlock or serialization failure and rolls back quietly.
- The orphan sweep skips tickers with a SELL filled or submitted in the last 30 minutes, or still trade_pending.

**Files changed:** `db.py` (AI-edited: `close_trade_outcome`, `flag_orphan_outcomes`), `tests/test_close_outcome_locking.py` (AI-generated), `tests/test_tasks_dedup.py` (AI-edited)

**Commit:** `08cc7b1`, `db66097`, `b587bd9`, `27e7e5c`, `da0ece0`

**Deploy:** next-2026-11-07 `da0ece0`, planned live from 11-09 05:00 (pending a decision on the weekend loads)

## 2026-11-04 17:04 EST (commit): A/B resume check, stale-quote None fix, concentration record

**Request (professor review run 49 (trading-bot-aa, an AI reviewer agent), relayed on the bus at 16:47; worked under the improve-loop cron prompt that Luke set up, run by Claude (trading-bot-ee); not a new prompt from Luke):**
> M290: the runner never compares a resume's config (model_digest, ollama_version, options, tool_sha) with the first run line ... M288: execution.py:1512-1517. If the first quote is stale and the last of the 3 retries returns None, pq becomes None. The stale-BUY defer/abort and the 8% drift check are then both skipped ... M289: the persisted concentration flag still writes "position X% exceeds cap" using max(target, order) ... crew.py doesn't rescale sizing["order_pct"] after the cap ... (excerpt)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: (1) a check that refuses a mismatched A/B resume without anyone reading arm data; (2) the stale-BUY guard and drift check survive a None quote; (3) the stored sizing says which weight the concentration check used and what the cap left.
> Scope / files: scripts/g33_resume_check.py (new, dev), execution.py and agents/crew.py (run47-plans), tests.
> Constraints: (1) must not change the runner (tool_sha hashes it, so an edit would split the resume) and reads only kind "run" lines; (2) no new block condition: with no priced quote at all, use the latest price as the existing exception path does; (3) flag text unchanged (downstream readers), record-only keys plus the order_pct rescale; nothing reaches the brief.
> Out of scope (do not touch): G33 design, sample or decision rules; stale-price retry policy (G49); cap values (#183/G36).
> Acceptance check: new tests fail on the old code and pass on the new; full suite passes; professor review.
> Open questions for Luke: none (whether a missing quote should defer like a stale one is a follow-up once logged).

**Done:**
- g33_resume_check.py compares run lines with the first and, with --preflight, the config a run started now would record. It exits 2 on a mismatch.
- A None on a stale re-check now keeps the last quote. With no priced quote at all, the drift check falls back to ibkr.get_latest_price and logs a warning.
- risk_json.concentration_check records checked_pct, target_pct, order_pct, cap_pct, shares_before, shares_after and order_pct_after. order_pct is now rescaled with the capped shares.

**Files changed:**
- `scripts/g33_resume_check.py` (AI-generated)
- `tests/test_g33_resume_check.py` (AI-generated)
- `execution.py` (AI-edited: priced-quote re-check loop in `execute_approved`)
- `tests/test_execution.py` (AI-edited: two tests in `TestStalePriceBuyGuard`)
- `agents/crew.py` (AI-edited: concentration block of `_route_to_approval`)
- `tests/test_concentration_record.py` (AI-generated)

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: after a TRIM or manual SELL, the broker stop has already been cancelled. If the sell part-fills, the code re-placed a stop for owned - filled while the rest of the sell was still working (over-stop once it fills); if the stop record read returned None (missing, or a DB error read non-strict), no branch ran and the remaining shares had no stop, no alert and no retry. Route both cases to the existing reconcile re-protect (which subtracts a working sell) and alert.
> Scope / files: execution.py (execute_approved TRIM branch, execute_manual SELL branch), tests/test_trim_remainder_protection.py (run47-plans).
> Constraints: EXIT's part-fill handling unchanged; a full fill with a readable record still re-places at once; strict record read.

> Acceptance check: part-filled TRIM and manual sell queue RESTORE and don't place; missing and unreadable records queue RESTORE and alert; a full fill with a record still places for the remainder; full suite passes.
> Open questions for Luke: none.

**Done:** both paths read the record strictly; a part fill or a missing/unreadable record queues tasks._queue_reprotect(RESTORE, order id) and sends one alert; execute_manual's local_order_id is initialised before its try. Five tests. Suite 4225 passed.

**Files changed:** `execution.py` (AI-edited: execute_approved TRIM stop block, execute_manual SELL stop block), `tests/test_trim_remainder_protection.py` (AI-generated)

Follow-up `4428661` (run47-plans, professor 62j and an SEF question): the queued restore reads a fresh price for the floor check and passes it to the stop placement, skipping the floor with no price; the floor applies after a profit target's stop and survives a merge into a queued target (`tasks._reprotect_after_late_sell`, `tasks._queue_reprotect`, AI-edited); tests added in `tests/test_trim_remainder_protection.py` (AI-generated). ## 2026-11-08 17:05 EST: Telegram-first shutdown, stop budget, bounded IB qualify, stop-lock socket timeouts

**Trigger:** limitations audit 2026-11-08 15:08, items #5 and #8. A review subagent then flagged that the first version's 15 s join was shorter than an approval, and that joining the long-polling poller wasted the wait.

**Request drafted by:** audit findings #5 and #8 (AI). Luke's standing permission covers changes like this.

**Open for Luke:** none. No root change was needed: the LLM grace dropped from 420 to 390 s instead of raising TimeoutStopSec.

**AI-refined (Claude):**
> Goal: no approval starts during a stop, a running one gets to finish before exit, and the whole stop fits TimeoutStopSec=480. IB contract qualification and stop-lock Redis calls can't hang forever.
> Scope / files: main.py, process_manager.py, ibkr.py, stop_lock.py, tests.
> Constraints: never raise in shutdown; keep stop-lock fail-open; keep orders-last (M57).
> Out of scope: unit-file edits (need root), order logic.
> Acceptance check: new tests including the budget check against the unit file, full suite green, fresh subagent review.
> Open questions for Luke: none

**Done:** `_shutdown` now calls `_stop_telegram_polling()` first, then `stop_all`, then `_join_telegram_worker` against a deadline 60 s from the start of the stop. Only the worker thread is joined, not the poller. The LLM grace is 390 s, so the worst case is 5 + 390 + 60 = 455 s. Pool children drain the commission writer at exit (`ibkr.drain_commission_queue`). `_stock` qualifies through `_bounded_req`. The stop-lock Redis client gets 5 s socket and connect timeouts, and a timeout takes the existing fail-open path.

**Files changed:** `main.py` (AI-edited: _TELEGRAM_JOIN_S, _stop_telegram_polling, _join_telegram_worker, _shutdown), `process_manager.py` (AI-edited: _CELERY_GRACE_S and comment), `ibkr.py` (AI-edited: _commission_writer task_done, drain_commission_queue, _stock), `tasks.py` (AI-edited: _drain_on_child_exit commission drain), `stop_lock.py` (AI-edited: _REDIS_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_S, _redis), `tests/test_shutdown_order.py`, `tests/test_qualify_bounded.py`, `tests/test_stop_lock_socket_timeout.py` (AI-generated), `tests/test_process_manager_stop.py`, `tests/test_child_exit_drain.py` (AI-edited)

**Done:** `stop_all(before_orders=)` hook. Shutdown waits there for the telegram-worker thread, until 180 s after the stop began.
